===== src/components/admin/ProductFormModal.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ref, watch } from 'vue'
import { X, Save } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { REAL_CATEGORIES } from '../../constants/categories.js'

const props = defineProps({ isOpen: Boolean, editingProduct: Object })
const emit = defineEmits(['close','save'])

const form = ref({ name: '', category: 'SNACK 1K', price_sell: 0, qty: 0, minStock: 2 })

watch(() => props.editingProduct, (p) => {
  if (p) {
    form.value = { name: p.name, category: p.category, price_sell: p.price, qty: p.stock, minStock: p.minStock }
  } else {
    form.value = { name: '', category: 'SNACK 1K', price_sell: 0, qty: 0, minStock: 2 }
  }
}, { immediate: true })

function save() {
  if (!form.value.name || form.value.price_sell <= 0) { alert('Nama dan harga wajib!'); return }
  emit('save', form.value)
}
</script>

<template>
  <Teleport to="body">
    <Transition name="modal">
      <div v-if="isOpen" class="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm" @click="emit('close')"></div>
        <div class="relative bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-2xl sm:rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-4 animate-slide-up max-h-[90vh] overflow-auto">
          <div class="flex justify-between items-center">
            <h2 class="font-bold text-lg">{{ editingProduct ? 'Edit Produk' : 'Tambah Produk' }}</h2>
            <button @click="emit('close')" class="bg-gray-100 p-1.5 rounded-full"><X :size="18" /></button>
          </div>
          <div class="space-y-3">
            <div><label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Nama Produk</label><input v-model="form.name" class="w-full border rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mt-1 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 outline-none" /></div>
            <div><label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Kategori</label><select v-model="form.category" class="w-full border rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mt-1"><option v-for="cat in REAL_CATEGORIES" :key="cat" :value="cat">{{ cat }}</option></select></div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3"><div><label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Harga</label><input v-model.number="form.price_sell" type="number" class="w-full border rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mt-1" /></div><div><label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Stok</label><input v-model.number="form.qty" type="number" class="w-full border rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mt-1" /></div></div>
            <div><label class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500">Min Stok</label><input v-model.number="form.minStock" type="number" class="w-full border rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm mt-1" /></div>
          </div>
          <button @click="save" class="w-full bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2"><Save :size="18" />{{ editingProduct ? 'Simpan' : 'Tambah' }}</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Transition>
  </Teleport>
</template>

<style scoped>
.modal-enter-active, .modal-leave-active { transition: opacity 0.25s ease; }
.modal-enter-from, .modal-leave-to { opacity: 0; }
.modal-enter-active .animate-slide-up { animation: slideUp 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1); }
@keyframes slideUp { from { transform: translateY(100%); } to { transform: translateY(0); } }
</style>

===== src/components/admin/ProductTable.vue =====
<script setup>
import { Edit2, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next'
const props = defineProps({ products: Array })
const emit = defineEmits(['edit','delete'])
</script>
<template>
  <div class="bg-white rounded-xl border overflow-hidden">
    <div class="overflow-x-auto">
      <table class="w-full text-sm">
        <thead class="bg-slate-50 text-xs text-slate-500"><tr><th class="text-left p-3">Produk</th><th class="text-left p-3">Kategori</th><th class="text-left p-3">Harga</th><th class="text-left p-3">Stok</th><th class="text-right p-3">Aksi</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          <tr v-for="p in products" :key="p.id" class="border-t hover:bg-slate-50">
            <td class="p-3"><p class="font-semibold">{{ p.name }}</p><p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">{{ p.id.substring(0,8) }}</p></td>
            <td class="p-3"><span class="text-xs bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{{ p.category }}</span></td>
            <td class="p-3 font-bold">Rp {{ p.price.toLocaleString('id-ID') }}</td>
            <td class="p-3"><span :class="['text-xs px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold', p.stock===0 ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-600' : p.isLowStock ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-600' : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600']">{{ p.stock }}</span></td>
            <td class="p-3"><div class="flex justify-end gap-1"><button @click="emit('edit', p)" class="bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 p-1.5 rounded-lg"><Edit2 :size="14"/></button><button @click="emit('delete', p)" class="bg-red-50 text-red-600 p-1.5 rounded-lg"><Trash2 :size="14"/></button></div></td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

===== src/components/catalog/CategoryFilter.vue =====
<script setup>
import { UtensilsCrossed, Coffee, CupSoda, Cookie, Pill, Sparkles, Candy, Snowflake, Soup, Heart, Wrench, Package, Flame, Cigarette } from 'lucide-vue-next'
const props = defineProps({ categories: { type: Array, default: () => [] }, selectedCategory: { type: String, default: 'SEMUA' } })
const emit = defineEmits(['select'])
const iconComponents = { UtensilsCrossed, Coffee, CupSoda, Cookie, Pill, Sparkles, Candy, Snowflake, Soup, Heart, Wrench, Package, Flame, Cigarette }
function selectCat(name) { emit('select', name) }
</script>
<template>
  <section>
    <h2 class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 mb-2.5 uppercase tracking-wider">Kategori ({{ categories.length }} Kategori)</h2>
    <div class="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 scrollbar-none">
      <button @click="selectCat('SEMUA')" :class="['text-xs font-medium px-4 py-2 rounded-full shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5', selectedCategory === 'SEMUA' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-white border text-slate-700']"><Package :size="14" /> Semua</button>
      <button v-for="cat in categories" :key="cat.id" @click="selectCat(cat.name)" :class="['text-xs font-medium px-3 py-2 rounded-full shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1.5', selectedCategory === cat.name ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow' : 'bg-white border text-slate-700']"><component :is="iconComponents[cat.icon] || Package" :size="14" />{{ cat.name }} ({{ cat.count }})</button>
    </div>
  </section>
</template>

===== src/components/catalog/ProductVariantModal.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { X, Flame, Snowflake, Coffee, UtensilsCrossed, Check } from 'lucide-vue-next'

const props = defineProps({
  product: { type: Object, required: true },
  isOpen: { type: Boolean, default: false }
})

const emit = defineEmits(['close', 'confirm'])

const selectedServiceId = ref(null)

function getIconForService(serviceName) {
  const name = serviceName.toLowerCase()
  if (name.includes('es') || name.includes('dingin') || name.includes('cekek')) return Snowflake
  if (name.includes('seduh') || name.includes('panas') || name.includes('teko')) return Flame
  if (name.includes('kopi') || name.includes('ice')) return Coffee
  return UtensilsCrossed
}

function selectService(id) {
  selectedServiceId.value = id
}

function confirm() {
  const service = selectedServiceId.value 
    ? props.product.availableServices.find(s => s.id === selectedServiceId.value)
    : null
  emit('confirm', service)
  selectedServiceId.value = null
  emit('close')
}

function close() {
  selectedServiceId.value = null
  emit('close')
}
</script>

<template>
  <Teleport to="body">
    <Transition name="modal">
      <div v-if="isOpen" class="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center">
        <!-- Backdrop -->
        <div class="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm" @click="close"></div>

        <!-- Modal Content - Bottom Sheet di mobile, Center di desktop -->
        <div class="relative bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-[20px] sm:rounded-[20px] shadow-2xl max-h-[80vh] flex flex-col animate-slide-up">
          <!-- Handle bar (mobile) -->
          <div class="flex justify-center pt-3 pb-2 sm:hidden">
            <div class="w-10 h-1 bg-gray-300 rounded-full"></div>
          </div>

          <!-- Header -->
          <div class="px-5 pb-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-start">
            <div class="flex-1">
              <h3 class="font-bold text-[16px] text-slate-800">{{ product.name }}</h3>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">{{ product.category }} • Stok {{ product.stock }} {{ product.unit }}</p>
            </div>
            <button @click="close" class="ml-3 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 p-2 rounded-full transition">
              <X :size="16" class="text-slate-600" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <!-- Options -->
          <div class="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-5 py-4 space-y-3">
            <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Pilih Penyajian</p>
            
            <!-- Opsi Mentah -->
            <button
              @click="selectService(null)"
              :class="[
                'w-full text-left p-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all flex items-center justify-between',
                selectedServiceId === null 
                  ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50' 
                  : 'border-gray-200 bg-white hover:border-gray-300'
              ]"
            >
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div :class="['p-2.5 rounded-full', selectedServiceId === null ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500']">
                  <UtensilsCrossed :size="18" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p class="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-800">Mentah - Bawa Pulang</p>
                  <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Tanpa diseduh, langsung bawa</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Rp {{ product.price.toLocaleString('id-ID') }}</span>
                <div v-if="selectedServiceId === null" class="bg-emerald-500 rounded-full p-0.5">
                  <Check :size="14" class="text-white" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </button>

            <!-- Opsi Service (Seduh, Es, dll) -->
            <button
              v-for="svc in product.availableServices"
              :key="svc.id"
              @click="selectService(svc.id)"
              :class="[
                'w-full text-left p-3.5 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all flex items-center justify-between',
                selectedServiceId === svc.id 
                  ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-50' 
                  : 'border-gray-200 bg-white hover:border-gray-300'
              ]"
            >
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div :class="['p-2.5 rounded-full', selectedServiceId === svc.id ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white' : 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-600']">
                  <component :is="getIconForService(svc.name)" :size="18" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p class="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-800">{{ svc.name }}</p>
                  <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-500">+Rp{{ svc.price.toLocaleString('id-ID') }} biaya servis</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Rp {{ (product.price + svc.price).toLocaleString('id-ID') }}</span>
                <div v-if="selectedServiceId === svc.id" class="bg-emerald-500 rounded-full p-0.5">
                  <Check :size="14" class="text-white" />
                </div>
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>

          <!-- Footer Action -->
          <div class="p-5 border-t border-gray-100 bg-white rounded-b-[20px]">
            <button 
              @click="confirm"
              class="w-full bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 active:scale-[0.98] text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl transition-all shadow-lg shadow-emerald-200"
            >
              Tambah ke Keranjang • Rp {{ 
                selectedServiceId 
                  ? (product.price + (product.availableServices.find(s=>s.id===selectedServiceId)?.price||0)).toLocaleString('id-ID')
                  : product.price.toLocaleString('id-ID')
              }}
            </button>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-center text-gray-400 mt-2.5">Pilihan servis sesuai PRD Warung Sinar Pagi</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </Transition>
  </Teleport>
</template>

<style scoped>
.modal-enter-active, .modal-leave-active {
  transition: opacity 0.25s ease;
}
.modal-enter-from, .modal-leave-to {
  opacity: 0;
}
.modal-enter-active .animate-slide-up {
  animation: slideUp 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);
}
.modal-leave-active .animate-slide-up {
  animation: slideDown 0.25s ease forwards;
}
@keyframes slideUp {
  from { transform: translateY(100%); }
  to { transform: translateY(0); }
}
@keyframes slideDown {
  from { transform: translateY(0); }
  to { transform: translateY(100%); }
}
</style>

===== src/components/catalog/ProductCard.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useCartStore } from '../../stores/cartStore'

const props = defineProps({
  product: { type: Object, required: true }
})

const cartStore = useCartStore()
const selectedServiceId = ref(null)

function getSelectedService() {
  if (!selectedServiceId.value) return null
  return props.product.availableServices?.find(s => s.id === selectedServiceId.value) || null
}

function handleAdd() {
  cartStore.addItem(props.product, getSelectedService())
  selectedServiceId.value = null
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="border rounded-xl p-3 bg-white shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
    <div>
      <div class="w-full h-32 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden mb-2 relative">
        <img 
          :src="product.imageUrl" 
          :alt="product.name" 
          class="w-full h-full object-cover"
          loading="lazy"
          @error="(e) => e.target.src = 'https://placehold.co/300x300?text=Sinar+Pagi'"
        />
        <span v-if="!product.isAvailable" class="absolute top-1 left-1 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">HABIS</span>
        <span v-else-if="product.isLowStock" class="absolute top-1 left-1 bg-amber-500 text-white text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">SISA {{ product.stock }}</span>
      </div>
      <span class="text-[10px] text-emerald-600 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide">{{ product.category }}</span>
      <h3 class="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800 line-clamp-2 leading-tight">{{ product.name }}</h3>
    </div>

    <div class="mt-2 space-y-2">
      <div>
        <p class="text-xs text-gray-800 font-bold">Rp {{ product.price.toLocaleString('id-ID') }} / {{ product.unit }}</p>
        <p class="text-[10px] text-gray-400">Stok: {{ product.stock }}</p>
      </div>

      <select 
        v-if="product.availableServices?.length > 0"
        v-model="selectedServiceId"
        class="w-full border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-[11px] p-1.5 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-300 outline-none"
      >
        <option :value="null">Mentah (bawa pulang)</option>
        <option v-for="svc in product.availableServices" :key="svc.id" :value="svc.id">
          {{ svc.name }} +Rp{{ svc.price.toLocaleString('id-ID') }}
        </option>
      </select>

      <button 
        @click="handleAdd"
        :disabled="!product.isAvailable"
        class="w-full bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:bg-gray-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition"
      >
        {{ product.isAvailable ? '+ Tambah' : 'Habis' }}
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

===== src/components/cart/CartItem.vue =====
<script setup>
import { CheckCircle2, Minus, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-vue-next'
const props = defineProps({ item: Object })
const emit = defineEmits(['decrease','increase','remove'])
function getTotal(item){ return (item.price + (item.selectedService?.price||0)) * item.quantity }
</script>
<template>
  <div class="flex gap-3 p-3 bg-slate-50 rounded-xl border border-slate-100">
    <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
      <p class="font-semibold text-sm truncate">{{ item.name }}</p>
      <p v-if="item.selectedService" class="text-[11px] text-amber-600 flex items-center gap-1"><CheckCircle2 :size="12"/>{{ item.selectedService.name }}</p>
      <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Rp {{ getTotal(item).toLocaleString('id-ID') }} ({{ item.quantity }}x)</p>
    </div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
      <button @click="emit('decrease', item)" class="w-7 h-7 bg-white border rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"><component :is="item.quantity<=1 ? Trash2 : Minus" :size="14" /></button>
      <span class="text-sm font-bold w-5 text-center">{{ item.quantity }}</span>
      <button @click="emit('increase', item)" class="w-7 h-7 bg-white border rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"><Plus :size="14" /></button>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

===== src/components/cart/CartDrawer.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted } from 'vue'
import { useCartStore } from '../../stores/cartStore'
import { ShoppingBag, MapPin, QrCode, Banknote, FileText, User } from 'lucide-vue-next'

const cartStore = useCartStore()
const isOpen = ref(false)
const customerInfo = ref({ name: '', kostName: '', room: '', paymentMethod: 'QRIS', notes: '' })

const STORE_PHONE = import.meta.env.VITE_STORE_PHONE || '089516477760'

onMounted(() => {
  const saved = cartStore.getSavedCustomer()
  if (saved) customerInfo.value = { ...customerInfo.value, ...saved }
})

function handleCheckout() {
  if (!customerInfo.value.name || !customerInfo.value.kostName || !customerInfo.value.room) {
    alert('Isi Nama, Nama Kost, dan No. Kamar dulu ya!')
    return
  }
  cartStore.checkoutViaWhatsApp(STORE_PHONE, customerInfo.value)
}
</script>

<template>
  <button 
    @click="isOpen = true"
    class="fixed bottom-4 right-4 left-4 mx-auto max-w-md bg-emerald-600 text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-full shadow-lg font-bold flex items-center justify-between z-40"
  >
    <span class="flex items-center gap-2"><ShoppingBag :size="18" /> {{ cartStore.totalItems }} Item</span>
    <span>Rp {{ cartStore.totalPrice.toLocaleString('id-ID') }} → Checkout</span>
  </button>

  <div v-if="isOpen" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex justify-end" @click.self="isOpen = false">
    <div class="bg-white w-full max-w-md h-full p-4 flex flex-col justify-between overflow-y-auto">
      <div>
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
          <h2 class="font-bold text-lg">Keranjang Belanja</h2>
          <button @click="isOpen = false" class="text-gray-500 text-xl font-bold">&times;</button>
        </div>

        <div v-if="cartStore.items.length === 0" class="text-center py-10 text-gray-400">Keranjang masih kosong nih.</div>

        <div v-else class="space-y-3 max-h-[40vh] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          <div v-for="item in cartStore.items" :key="item.cartKey" class="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-2">
            <div class="flex-1">
              <p class="font-semibold text-sm">{{ item.name }}</p>
              <p class="text-[11px] text-amber-600" v-if="item.selectedService">{{ item.selectedService.name }} +Rp{{ item.selectedService.price.toLocaleString() }}</p>
              <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Rp {{ item.price.toLocaleString('id-ID') }} x {{ item.quantity }}</p>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button @click="cartStore.updateQuantity(item.cartKey, item.quantity - 1)" class="px-2 bg-gray-200 rounded">-</button>
              <span class="text-sm font-bold">{{ item.quantity }}</span>
              <button @click="cartStore.updateQuantity(item.cartKey, item.quantity + 1)" class="px-2 bg-gray-200 rounded">+</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div v-if="cartStore.items.length > 0" class="border-t pt-4 space-y-3">
        <div class="flex justify-between font-bold text-base">
          <span>Total:</span><span>Rp {{ cartStore.totalPrice.toLocaleString('id-ID') }}</span>
        </div>

        <div class="space-y-2">
          <div class="relative">
            <User class="absolute left-2 top-2.5 text-gray-400" :size="16" />
            <input v-model="customerInfo.name" placeholder="Nama Pemesan (Wajib)" class="w-full border p-2 pl-8 rounded text-sm" />
          </div>
          <div class="relative">
            <MapPin class="absolute left-2 top-2.5 text-gray-400" :size="16" />
            <input v-model="customerInfo.kostName" placeholder="Nama Kost (Wajib) ex: Wisma Asri" class="w-full border p-2 pl-8 rounded text-sm" />
          </div>
          <input v-model="customerInfo.room" placeholder="No. Kamar (Wajib) ex: Kamar 104" class="w-full border p-2 rounded text-sm" />
          
          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <button @click="customerInfo.paymentMethod = 'QRIS'" :class="customerInfo.paymentMethod === 'QRIS' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-white border'" class="flex-1 py-2 rounded text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1"><QrCode :size="14" /> QRIS</button>
            <button @click="customerInfo.paymentMethod = 'COD'" :class="customerInfo.paymentMethod === 'COD' ? 'bg-emerald-600 text-white' : 'bg-white border'" class="flex-1 py-2 rounded text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1"><Banknote :size="14" /> Cash COD</button>
          </div>

          <div class="relative">
            <FileText class="absolute left-2 top-2.5 text-gray-400" :size="16" />
            <textarea v-model="customerInfo.notes" placeholder="Catatan (Opsional) ex: Kopinya agak manis" class="w-full border p-2 pl-8 rounded text-sm h-16"></textarea>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button @click="handleCheckout" class="w-full bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold">Kirim Pesanan ke WhatsApp</button>
        <p class="text-[10px] text-gray-400 text-center">Data kost akan disimpan otomatis untuk pesanan berikutnya</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

===== src/stores/catalogStore.js =====
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
import { productService } from '../services/product/service.js'

export const useCatalogStore = defineStore('catalog', () => {
  const products = ref([])
  const categories = ref([])
  const selectedCategory = ref('SEMUA')
  const searchQuery = ref('')
  const isLoading = ref(false)

  async function fetchCatalog() {
    isLoading.value = true
    try {
      products.value = await productService.getAllProducts()
      categories.value = await productService.getCategories()
      categories.value.sort((a,b) => (b.count||0) - (a.count||0))
    } finally {
      isLoading.value = false
    }
  }

  const filteredProducts = computed(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.value.toLowerCase().trim()
    return products.value.filter(p => {
      const matchCat = selectedCategory.value === 'SEMUA' || p.category === selectedCategory.value
      const matchSearch = !q || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      return matchCat && matchSearch && p.isAvailable
    })
  })

  const totalCount = computed(() => categories.value.reduce((a,c) => a + (c.count||0), 0))

  function setCategory(name) { selectedCategory.value = name }

  return { products, categories, selectedCategory, searchQuery, isLoading, filteredProducts, totalCount, fetchCatalog, setCategory }
})

===== src/stores/cartStore.js =====
import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
import { ref, computed, watch } from 'vue'
import { whatsappService } from '../services/whatsapp/service.js'

const CART_KEY = 'sinar-pagi-cart-v2'
const CUSTOMER_KEY = 'sinar-pagi-customer-v2'

export const useCartStore = defineStore('cart', () => {
  const items = ref(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CART_KEY) || '[]'))

  const totalItems = computed(() => items.value.reduce((s,i) => s + i.quantity, 0))
  const totalPrice = computed(() => items.value.reduce((s,i) => {
    const sp = i.selectedService?.price || 0
    return s + ((i.price + sp) * i.quantity)
  }, 0))

  watch(items, v => localStorage.setItem(CART_KEY, JSON.stringify(v)), { deep: true })

  function addItem(product, selectedService = null) {
    if (!product.isAvailable || product.stock <= 0) { alert('Stok habis!'); return }
    const key = `${product.id}-${selectedService?.id || 'no-service'}`
    const existing = items.value.find(i => i.cartKey === key)
    if (existing) {
      if (existing.quantity >= product.stock) { alert(`Stok ${product.name} cuma ${product.stock}`); return }
      existing.quantity++
    } else {
      items.value.push({ cartKey: key, id: product.id, name: product.name, price: product.price, unit: product.unit, stock: product.stock, selectedService, quantity: 1 })
    }
  }

  function removeItem(cartKey) { items.value = items.value.filter(i => i.cartKey !== cartKey) }
  function updateQuantity(cartKey, qty) {
    const item = items.value.find(i => i.cartKey === cartKey)
    if (!item) return
    if (qty <= 0) return removeItem(cartKey)
    if (qty > item.stock) { alert(`Maksimal ${item.stock}`); return }
    item.quantity = qty
  }
  function checkoutViaWhatsApp(phone, customerInfo) {
    const msg = whatsappService.formatOrderMessage(items.value, customerInfo)
    const url = whatsappService.generateWhatsAppURL(phone, msg)
    localStorage.setItem(CUSTOMER_KEY, JSON.stringify(customerInfo))
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }
  function getSavedCustomer() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CUSTOMER_KEY) || 'null') } catch { return null } }
  function clearCart() { items.value = [] }

  return { items, totalItems, totalPrice, addItem, removeItem, updateQuantity, checkoutViaWhatsApp, getSavedCustomer, clearCart }
})

===== src/composables/useCart.js =====
import { useCartStore } from '../stores/cartStore.js'
import { storeToRefs } from 'pinia'

export function useCart() {
  const store = useCartStore()
  const { items, totalItems, totalPrice } = storeToRefs(store)
  return { ...store, items, totalItems, totalPrice }
}

===== src/composables/useCatalog.js =====
import { onMounted } from 'vue'
import { storeToRefs } from 'pinia'
import { useCatalogStore } from '../stores/catalogStore.js'
import { productService } from '../services/product/service.js'
import fullDB from '../mock/full-db.json'

export function useCatalog() {
  const store = useCatalogStore()
  const { categories, selectedCategory, searchQuery, filteredProducts, isLoading, totalCount } = storeToRefs(store)
  const { setCategory, fetchCatalog } = store

  onMounted(async () => {
    try {
      await productService.initFromFullDB(fullDB)
      await fetchCatalog()
    } catch (e) { console.error('Gagal memuat katalog:', e) }
  })

  return { categories, selectedCategory, searchQuery, filteredProducts, isLoading, totalCount, setCategory, fetchCatalog }
}

===== src/services/admin/productAdminService.js =====
import { db } from '../../db/index.js'

export const productAdminService = {
  async getAll() {
    const all = await db.products.toArray()
    return all.sort((a,b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
  },

  async search(query) {
    const all = await this.getAll()
    if (!query) return all
    const q = query.toLowerCase()
    return all.filter(p => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.category.toLowerCase().includes(q))
  },

  async create(payload) {
    const newProduct = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      name: payload.name,
      code: '',
      category: payload.category,
      categoryIcon: 'Package',
      price: payload.price_sell,
      unit: 'PCS',
      stock: payload.qty,
      minStock: payload.minStock,
      isAvailable: payload.qty > 0,
      isLowStock: payload.qty <= payload.minStock && payload.qty > 0,
      availableServices: [],
      imageUrl: `https://placehold.co/300x300/e2e8f0/475569?text=${encodeURIComponent(payload.name.substring(0,12))}`,
      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    }
    await db.products.add(newProduct)
    return newProduct
  },

  async update(id, payload) {
    await db.products.update(id, {
      name: payload.name,
      category: payload.category,
      price: payload.price_sell,
      stock: payload.qty,
      minStock: payload.minStock,
      isAvailable: payload.qty > 0,
      isLowStock: payload.qty <= payload.minStock && payload.qty > 0
    })
  },

  async remove(id) {
    await db.products.delete(id)
  },

  getStats(products) {
    return {
      total: products.length,
      lowStock: products.filter(p => p.isLowStock).length,
      outOfStock: products.filter(p => !p.isAvailable).length,
      categories: 21
    }
  }
}

===== src/services/admin/authService.js =====
import { db } from '../../db/index.js'

const DEFAULT_PINS = [
  { id: '300993', name: 'Septian Pratama', pin: '300993' },
  { id: '081194', name: 'Aniyatu Solihah', pin: '081194' }
]

export const authService = {
  async getValidPins() {
    try {
      const operators = await db.operators.toArray()
      if (operators.length > 0) return operators.map(o => o.pin)
    } catch {}
    return DEFAULT_PINS.map(o => o.pin)
  },

  async login(pin) {
    const validPins = await this.getValidPins()
    if (validPins.includes(pin)) {
      localStorage.setItem('sinar-pagi-admin', 'true')
      localStorage.setItem('sinar-pagi-operator', pin)
      return true
    }
    return false
  },

  logout() {
    localStorage.removeItem('sinar-pagi-admin')
    localStorage.removeItem('sinar-pagi-operator')
  },

  isLoggedIn() {
    return localStorage.getItem('sinar-pagi-admin') === 'true'
  }
}

===== src/services/product/transformer.js =====
import { CATEGORY_ICON_MAP } from '../../constants/categories.js'

export function transformPOSProduct(rawProduct, allServices = []) {
  const activeServices = allServices.filter(s => !s.deleted)
  const availableServices = activeServices.filter(s => {
    return s.category === rawProduct.category ||
           (rawProduct.category.includes('MIE') && s.category.includes('MIE')) ||
           (rawProduct.category === 'POP ICE' && s.category === 'POP ICE')
  })

  return {
    id: rawProduct.id,
    name: rawProduct.name,
    code: rawProduct.code || '',
    category: rawProduct.category || 'UMUM',
    categoryIcon: CATEGORY_ICON_MAP[rawProduct.category] || 'Package',
    price: rawProduct.price_sell || 0,
    unit: rawProduct.unit || 'PCS',
    stock: rawProduct.qty || 0,
    minStock: rawProduct.minStock || 2,
    isLowStock: (rawProduct.qty || 0) <= (rawProduct.minStock || 2) && (rawProduct.qty || 0) > 0,
    isAvailable: (rawProduct.qty || 0) > 0,
    availableServices,
    imageUrl: `https://placehold.co/300x300/e2e8f0/475569?text=${encodeURIComponent(rawProduct.name.substring(0,12))}`,
    updatedAt: rawProduct.updatedAt
  }
}

===== src/services/product/service.js =====
import { transformPOSProduct } from './transformer.js'
import { productRepository } from './repository.js'
import { db } from '../../db/index.js'

export const productService = {
  async initFromFullDB(fullDB) {
    const count = await productRepository.count()
    if (count > 0) return

    const activeServices = (fullDB.services || []).filter(s => !s.deleted)
    if (activeServices.length) await productRepository.bulkAddServices(activeServices)

    const transformed = (fullDB.products || []).map(p => transformPOSProduct(p, activeServices))
    await productRepository.bulkAddProducts(transformed)

    const catMap = {}
    transformed.forEach(p => {
      if (!catMap[p.category]) {
        catMap[p.category] = { id: p.category, name: p.category, icon: p.categoryIcon, count: 0 }
      }
      catMap[p.category].count++
    })
    await productRepository.bulkAddCategories(Object.values(catMap))

    if (fullDB.settings?.[0]) {
      await db.settings.put({ id: 'app_config', ...fullDB.settings[0] })
    }

    if (fullDB.operators) {
      await db.operators.bulkPut(fullDB.operators.map(o => ({ id: o.id || o.pin, name: o.name, pin: o.pin })))
    }
  },

  async getAllProducts() { return await productRepository.getAll() },
  async getCategories() { return await productRepository.getCategories() },
  async getSettings() { return await productRepository.getSettings() }
}

===== src/services/product/repository.js =====
import { db } from '../../db/index.js'

export const productRepository = {
  async count() { return await db.products.count() },
  async getAll() { return await db.products.toArray() },
  async getCategories() { return await db.categories.toArray() },
  async getServices() { return await db.services.toArray() },
  async getSettings() { return await db.settings.get('app_config') },
  async bulkAddProducts(products) { return await db.products.bulkAdd(products) },
  async bulkAddCategories(categories) { return await db.categories.bulkAdd(categories) },
  async bulkAddServices(services) { return await db.services.bulkAdd(services) },
  async clearAll() {
    await db.products.clear()
    await db.categories.clear()
    await db.services.clear()
  }
}

===== src/services/whatsapp/service.js =====
import { to62 } from '../../utils/phone.js'
import { formatOrderMessage } from './formatter.js'

export const whatsappService = {
  formatOrderMessage,
  generateWhatsAppURL(phoneNumber, formattedMessage) {
    return `https://wa.me/${to62(phoneNumber)}?text=${formattedMessage}`
  }
}

===== src/services/whatsapp/formatter.js =====
export function formatOrderMessage(cartItems, customerInfo = {}) {
  const { name = 'Anak Kost', kostName = '-', room = '-', paymentMethod = 'QRIS', notes = '' } = customerInfo
  let text = `*PESANAN WARUNG SINAR PAGI*\n`
  text += `----------------------------------\n`
  text += `*Nama:* ${name}\n`
  text += `*Lokasi:* ${kostName} - ${room}\n`
  text += `*Pembayaran:* ${paymentMethod}\n\n`
  text += `*Daftar Pesanan:*\n`
  let total = 0
  cartItems.forEach(item => {
    const servicePrice = item.selectedService?.price || 0
    const subtotal = (item.price + servicePrice) * item.quantity
    total += subtotal
    let label = item.selectedService ? ` (${item.selectedService.name})` : ''
    text += `• ${item.quantity}x ${item.name}${label} - Rp${subtotal.toLocaleString('id-ID')}\n`
  })
  text += `\n----------------------------------\n`
  text += `*Total Belanja:* Rp ${total.toLocaleString('id-ID')}\n`
  if (notes) text += `\n*Catatan:* ${notes}\n`
  text += `\nMohon konfirmasi pesanan ya. Terima kasih!`
  return encodeURIComponent(text)
}

import { db } from '../db/index'

export const productService = {

      return s.category === rawProduct.category || 
             (rawProduct.category.includes('MIE') && s.category.includes('MIE')) ||
             (rawProduct.category === 'POP ICE' && s.category === 'POP ICE')
    })

    return {
      id: rawProduct.id,
      name: rawProduct.name,
      code: rawProduct.code || '',
      category: rawProduct.category || 'UMUM',
      categoryIcon: CATEGORY_ICON_MAP[rawProduct.category] || 'Package',
      price: rawProduct.price_sell || 0,
      unit: rawProduct.unit || 'PCS',
      stock: rawProduct.qty || 0,
      minStock: rawProduct.minStock || 2,
      isLowStock: (rawProduct.qty || 0) <= (rawProduct.minStock || 2) && (rawProduct.qty || 0) > 0,
      isAvailable: (rawProduct.qty || 0) > 0,
      availableServices,
      imageUrl: `https://placehold.co/300x300/e2e8f0/475569?text=${encodeURIComponent(rawProduct.name.substring(0,12))}`,
      updatedAt: rawProduct.updatedAt
    }
  },

  async initFromFullDB(fullDB) {
    const count = await db.products.count()
    if (count > 0) return

    const activeServices = (fullDB.services || []).filter(s => !s.deleted)
    await db.services.bulkAdd(activeServices)

    const transformed = (fullDB.products || []).map(p => this.transformPOSProduct(p, activeServices))
    await db.products.bulkAdd(transformed)

    const catMap = {}
    transformed.forEach(p => {
      if (!catMap[p.category]) {
        catMap[p.category] = { id: p.category, name: p.category, icon: p.categoryIcon, count: 0 }
      }
      catMap[p.category].count++

    if (fullDB.settings?.[0]) {
      await db.settings.put({ id: 'app_config', ...fullDB.settings[0] })
    }
  },

  async getAllProducts() { return await db.products.toArray() },
  async getCategories() { return await db.categories.toArray() },
  async getSettings() { return await db.settings.get('app_config') }
}

===== src/services/whatsappService.js =====
export const whatsappService = {
  formatOrderMessage(cartItems, customerInfo = {}) {
    const { 
      name = 'Anak Kost', 
      kostName = '-', 
      room = '-', 
      paymentMethod = 'QRIS',
      notes = '' 
    } = customerInfo
    
    let text = `*PESANAN WARUNG SINAR PAGI*\n`
    text += `----------------------------------\n`
    text += `*Nama:* ${name}\n`
    text += `*Lokasi:* ${kostName} - ${room}\n`
    text += `*Pembayaran:* ${paymentMethod}\n\n`

    text += `*Daftar Pesanan:*\n`
    let total = 0
    cartItems.forEach((item) => {
      const servicePrice = item.selectedService?.price || 0
      const itemPrice = item.price + servicePrice
      const subtotal = itemPrice * item.quantity
      total += subtotal

      let serviceLabel = item.selectedService ? ` (${item.selectedService.name})` : ''
      text += `• ${item.quantity}x ${item.name}${serviceLabel} - Rp${subtotal.toLocaleString('id-ID')}\n`
    })

    text += `\n----------------------------------\n`
    text += `*Total Belanja:* Rp ${total.toLocaleString('id-ID')}\n`

    if (notes) {
      text += `\n*Catatan:* ${notes}\n`
    }

    text += `\nMohon konfirmasi pesanan ya. Terima kasih!`

    return encodeURIComponent(text)
  },

  generateWhatsAppURL(phoneNumber, formattedMessage) {
    let cleanPhone = phoneNumber.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')
    if (cleanPhone.startsWith('0')) {
      cleanPhone = '62' + cleanPhone.substring(1)
    }
    return `https://wa.me/${cleanPhone}?text=${formattedMessage}`
  }
}

===== src/utils/phone.js =====
export function to62(phone) {
  let clean = phone.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '')
  if (clean.startsWith('0')) clean = '62' + clean.substring(1)
  return clean
}

===== src/utils/currency.js =====
export function formatRp(value) {
  return `Rp ${Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID')}`
}

export function formatRpShort(value) {
  const num = Number(value || 0)
  if (num >= 1000) return `Rp ${(num/1000).toFixed(0)}k`
  return formatRp(num)
}

===== src/router/index.js =====
import { createRouter, createWebHistory } from 'vue-router'

const routes = [
  {
    path: '/',
    name: 'Catalog',
    component: () => import('../views/CatalogView.vue')
  },
  {
    path: '/admin',
    name: 'AdminLogin',
    component: () => import('../views/admin/AdminLogin.vue')
  },
  {
    path: '/admin/dashboard',
    name: 'AdminDashboard',
    component: () => import('../views/admin/AdminDashboard.vue'),
    meta: { requiresAdmin: true }
  }
]

const router = createRouter({
  history: createWebHistory(),
  routes
})

router.beforeEach((to, from, next) => {
  const isAdmin = localStorage.getItem('sinar-pagi-admin') === 'true'
  if (to.meta.requiresAdmin && !isAdmin) {
    next('/admin')
  } else {
    next()
  }
})

export default router

===== src/db/index.js =====
import Dexie from 'dexie'

export const db = new Dexie('SinarPagiCatalogDB')

db.version(1).stores({
  products: 'id, name, category, price_sell, qty',
  categories: 'id, name'
})

db.version(2).stores({
  products: 'id, name, category, price, stock, isAvailable, minStock, updatedAt',
  categories: 'id, name, count, icon',
  services: 'id, name, category, price',
  settings: 'id',
  operators: 'id, name, pin'
}).upgrade(tx => tx.table('products').clear())

db.version(3).stores({
  products: 'id, name, category, price, stock, isAvailable, minStock, updatedAt',
  categories: 'id, name, count, icon',
  services: 'id, name, category, price',
  settings: 'id',
  operators: 'id, name, pin'
})

===== src/views/admin/AdminLogin.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { authService } from '../../services/admin/authService.js'
import { Lock } from 'lucide-vue-next'

const router = useRouter()
const pin = ref('')
const error = ref('')
const loading = ref(false)

async function login() {
  loading.value = true
  error.value = ''
  const ok = await authService.login(pin.value)
  if (ok) router.push('/admin/dashboard')
  else error.value = 'PIN salah! Cek PIN Kasir POS (300993 / 081194)'
  loading.value = false
}
</script>

<template>
  <div class="min-h-screen bg-slate-900 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-sm shadow-2xl">
      <div class="text-center mb-6">
        <div class="bg-emerald-100 w-14 h-14 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3"><Lock class="text-emerald-600" :size="28" /></div>
        <h1 class="text-xl font-bold">Admin Sinar Pagi</h1>
        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">PIN dari tabel operators</p>
      </div>
      <div class="space-y-4">
        <input v-model="pin" type="password" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="PIN (300993)" class="w-full border-2 border-slate-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm focus:border-emerald-500 outline-none" @keyup.enter="login" />
        <p v-if="error" class="text-xs text-red-500 bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-lg">{{ error }}</p>
        <button @click="login" :disabled="loading" class="w-full bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl">{{ loading ? '...' : 'Masuk' }}</button>
        <p class="text-[11px] text-center text-slate-400">Default: 300993 / 081194</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</template>

===== src/views/admin/AdminDashboard.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ref, onMounted, computed } from 'vue'
import { useRouter } from 'vue-router'
import { authService } from '../../services/admin/authService.js'
import { productAdminService } from '../../services/admin/productAdminService.js'
import StatsCards from '../../components/admin/StatsCards.vue'
import ProductTable from '../../components/admin/ProductTable.vue'
import ProductFormModal from '../../components/admin/ProductFormModal.vue'
import { LogOut, Plus, Search } from 'lucide-vue-next'

const router = useRouter()
const products = ref([])
const filtered = ref([])
const searchQuery = ref('')
const showForm = ref(false)
const editingProduct = ref(null)

const stats = computed(() => productAdminService.getStats(products.value))

onMounted(async () => { await load() })

async function load() {
  products.value = await productAdminService.getAll()
  applyFilter()
}

function applyFilter() {
  if (!searchQuery.value) filtered.value = products.value
  else filtered.value = products.value.filter(p => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.value.toLowerCase()))
}

function openAdd() { editingProduct.value = null; showForm.value = true }
function openEdit(p) { editingProduct.value = p; showForm.value = true }

async function handleSave(form) {
  if (editingProduct.value) await productAdminService.update(editingProduct.value.id, form)
  else await productAdminService.create(form)
  showForm.value = false
  await load()
}

async function handleDelete(p) {
  if (!confirm(`Hapus ${p.name}?`)) return
  await productAdminService.remove(p.id)
  await load()
}

function logout() { authService.logout(); router.push('/admin') }
</script>

<template>
  <div class="min-h-screen bg-slate-50">
    <header class="bg-slate-900 text-white p-4 sticky top-0 z-10">
      <div class="max-w-5xl mx-auto flex justify-between items-center">
        <div><h1 class="font-bold text-lg">Admin Dashboard</h1><p class="text-xs text-slate-400">{{ stats.total }} produk • {{ stats.lowStock }} hampir habis</p></div>
        <div class="flex gap-2"><button @click="$router.push('/')" class="bg-slate-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs">Katalog</button><button @click="logout" class="bg-red-600 p-2 rounded-lg"><LogOut :size="16" /></button></div>
      </div>
    </header>
    <main class="max-w-5xl mx-auto p-4 space-y-4">
      <StatsCards :stats="stats" />
      <div class="flex gap-2">
        <div class="relative flex-1"><Search class="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" :size="18" /><input v-model="searchQuery" @input="applyFilter" placeholder="Cari produk..." class="w-full bg-white border rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-sm" /></div>
        <button @click="openAdd" class="bg-emerald-600 text-white px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5"><Plus :size="16" /> Tambah</button>
      </div>
      <ProductTable :products="filtered" @edit="openEdit" @delete="handleDelete" />
    </main>
    <ProductFormModal :isOpen="showForm" :editingProduct="editingProduct" @close="showForm=false" @save="handleSave" />
  </div>
</template>

===== src/views/CatalogView.vue =====
<script setup>
import { ShoppingBag, Search } from 'lucide-vue-next'
import { useCatalog } from '../composables/useCatalog'
import ProductCard from '../components/catalog/ProductCard.vue'
import CategoryFilter from '../components/catalog/CategoryFilter.vue'
import CartDrawer from '../components/cart/CartDrawer.vue'

const { categories, selectedCategory, searchQuery, filteredProducts, isLoading, totalCount, setCategory } = useCatalog()
</script>

<template>
  <div class="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 text-slate-800 font-sans pb-24">
    <!-- HEADER DI LUAR MAIN, BIAR STICKY BENER -->
    <header class="sticky top-0 z-10 bg-emerald-600 text-white shadow-md p-4">
      <div class="max-w-md mx-auto flex justify-between items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 class="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight">Warung Sinar Pagi</h1>
          <p class="text-xs text-emerald-100">Ciheuleut Jl. Talas Ujung No. 38</p>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-emerald-700 p-2 rounded-full">
          <ShoppingBag :size="20" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>

    <main class="max-w-md mx-auto p-4 space-y-5">
      <div class="relative">
        <Search class="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" :size="18" />
        <input v-model="searchQuery" placeholder="Cari produk..." class="w-full bg-white border rounded-xl pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-500 outline-none shadow-sm" />
      </div>

      <CategoryFilter :categories="categories" :selectedCategory="selectedCategory" @select="setCategory" />

      <section>
        <div v-if="isLoading" class="text-center py-10 text-slate-400 text-sm">Memuat produk...</div>
        <div v-else-if="filteredProducts.length === 0" class="text-center py-10 text-slate-400 text-sm">Produk tidak ditemukan.</div>
        <div v-else class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <ProductCard v-for="product in filteredProducts" :key="product.id" :product="product" />
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>

    <CartDrawer />
  </div>
</template>
